=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def dp(self,i,s,prev,k,ct,n,dct):
        if k<0:
            return float("infinity")
        if i>=n:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            return x
        if (i,prev,ct,k) in dct:
            return dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]
        if s[i]==prev:
            inc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k,ct+1,n,dct)
        else:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            inc=x+self.dp(i+1,s,s[i],k,1,n,dct)
        exc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k-1,ct,n,dct)
        dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]='min(inc,exc)
        return min(inc,exc)

    def getLengthOfOptimalCompression(self, s: str, k: int) -> int:
        n=len(s)
        return self.dp(0,s,"",k,0,n,{})

```python
class Solution:
    def dp(self,i,s,prev,k,ct,n,dct):
        if k<0:
            return float("infinity")
        if i>=n:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            return x
        if (i,prev,ct,k) in dct:
            return dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]
        if s[i]==prev:
            inc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k,ct+1,n,dct)
        else:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            inc=x+self.dp(i+1,s,s[i],k,1,n,dct)
        exc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k-1,ct,n,dct)
        dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]='min(inc,exc)
        return min(inc,exc)

    def getLengthOfOptimalCompression(self, s: str, k: int) -> int:
        n=len(s)
        return self.dp(0,s,"",k,0,n,{})
```

        if k<0:
            return float("infinity")

Potential issue: The code's `dp` method's

=== FIXED CODE ===
class Solution:
    def dp(self,i,s,prev,k,ct,n,dct):
        if k<0:
            return float("infinity")
        if i>=n:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            return x
        if (i,prev,ct,k) in dct:
            return dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]
        if s[i]==prev:
            inc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k,ct+1,n,dct)
        else:
            x=0
            if ct>1:
                x=len(str(ct))+1
            elif ct==1:
                x=1
            inc=x+self.dp(i+1,s,s[i],k,1,n,dct)
        exc=self.dp(i+1,s,prev,k-1,ct,n,dct)
        dct[(i,prev,ct,k)]='min(inc,exc)
        return min(inc,exc)

    def getLengthOfOptimalCompression(self, s: str, k: int) -> int:
        n=len(s)
        return self.dp(0,s,"",k,0,n,{})
